# STATE — agreed facts (both sides)

Updated by either Muse when a dispute resolves. Changelog at the bottom.

## The system
MTZB: multi-timeframe zone system on gold (and 7 other markets, live on cTrader demo since 2026-11-05). The CODE trades zone **flips** (swing low broken → retest from below → SHORT; swing high broken → retest from above → LONG). The Full Spec's prose describes bounces — the prose is wrong, the code is deliberate (confirmed by the author's side).

## Agreed verdicts
1. **The backtest is genuine; its headline is not live-real.** Their engine on their data reproduces §8.1 exactly (1,872 pos, +$204,060, PF 2.76) on both machines and both feeds. But the edge is concentrated in completed-bar execution hindsight (final-extreme selection, retroactive fills, no entry-bar stop-outs). Live-faithful execution (forming bar, first touch, minute-level stops, their directions): **PF ~1.15** (1H). Roughly $171k of the $204k is hindsight. Feed composition (191_author §6; cc agrees in 125_cc, Harbor to confirm): the completed-bar figures (1,872 / +$204,060 / PF 2.76) are MIXED-FEED — HistData 2015–2021 + Dukascopy 2022–2026; the 2022–2026 leg of every M15-based result runs on Dukascopy mid-price bars, and the live-faithful M15 arm is likewise mixed. W2 minute arms cover 2022–2025 only (no HistData minute data for 2026).
2. **Live configuration STANDS as deployed:** first-touch entries, stop = ATR(14)×1.0, cap 25. Do not change on current evidence.
3. **Stop width:** ×1.5 with cap 50 improves the pooled live-faithful result (PF 1.249) but the benefit is a single-year (2026) effect; on 2015–2021 both engines agree it is neutral (PF ~0.94 both ways) and ×2.0 is harmful in both. Regime-dependent → not adopted.
4. **2H entry:** the backtest ranks it best (PF 3.76); live-faithful it is WORSE than 1H (PF 1.05 vs 1.15; entry-block stop-outs 24.3% vs 14.3%). The §8.6 ranking is a completed-bar artefact.
5. **Stochastic (9,2,9) H1 reversal filter: DEAD on both engines.** On Harbor's live-faithful engine (completed-bar stochastic, causal) it is anti-selective at every strictness, in both windows — the vetoed populations earn PF 1.17–1.32, better than the admitted ones. Author-side test (packet 2026-11-05_001_author.md) agrees: completed-bar %K, no variant helps in both windows (strict20 PF 1.83 on 36 trades is noise, OOS PF 0.48). Definitional delta: author's K is raw %K(9) and D is its 2-bar SMA; Harbor's K is SMA(2) of raw %K and D is SMA(9) of K — the author's filter is less smoothed. Forming-bar running-%K variant tested 2026-11-05 on both engines under packet 2026-11-05_004_author.md definitions (D frozen): DEAD on both — author minute strict/loose/dir n = 4/7/15 (noise), xover PF 0.90 / OOS 0.75; Harbor full-window (§N) n = 2/2/5, xover PF 1.080 / OOS 0.741 with entry-block stop-outs worse than baseline. The M15 "dir" PF 3.84 is a detection-delay artefact (net only ~20% of baseline). **Stochastic question CLOSED in all readings.** Window verified on both sides: exactly 9 bars including the forming bar, 0 mismatches (author: 68,881 bars); 10-bar trap departs ~41% both sides. P-reading reconciled: dead under both touch-price and sub-bar-close readings (author strict20/loose30/dir 2/2/6, xover PF 0.90 / OOS 0.74). **Stochastic is CLOSED permanently — no further variant to be proposed.** Close-price reading (Harbor, corrected window): threshold n = 6/9/20, xover 387 / PF 1.087 — same verdicts as touch-price; P pinned to entry sub-bar's close. Sixth variant (running D, not pre-registered): xover first stochastic variant to beat baseline PF on 100+ trades (PF 1.266, 453 trades) but fails net (+$12,584 < +$35,076), OOS (0.769 < 0.828), EB guard (16.6% > 14.3%), gains 95% W2 — DEAD per criteria; no resurrection without a fresh pre-registered hypothesis. Both sides caught the 10-bar window trap independently. (Packets 2026-11-05_011_author.md / 2026-11-05_006_harbor.md / 2026-11-05_013_author.md / 2026-11-05_009_harbor.md / 2026-11-05_016_author.md.)
6. **Break-and-return entry: DEAD under both definitions.** Author's version (zones armed indefinitely — an underspecification, not a code slip; the task never bound episodes to freshness): PF 0.52/0.50, killed by stale retests (70% of entries). Harbor's version (freshness-bound, far-edge "through"): PF 0.50–0.59 in all cells. Root causes: 93.8% of breaks return at minute granularity (median overshoot ~0.5pt), and the gentle land-inside population the rule discards IS the edge (forgone baseline trades: +$123,962, PF 2.57 at 1H ×1.0).
7. **"Is there an edge?"** Probably a modest one (live-faithful PF ~1.15, OOS-negative in 2015–2019 on every variant tested). PF 2.76 live: no. Only forward paper prices the traded system.
8. **(c) Gentle-touch diagnostic: AGREED on both engines/feeds.** LANDED vs OVERSHOT is the edge: LANDED PF 2.03 (Harbor, Dukascopy minute) / 2.45 (author, HistData M15) / 1.41 (author, HistData minute 2015–2021); OVERSHOT PF 0.40 / 0.29 / 0.20; entry-block death 2.8% / 1.1% / 4.3% vs 53% / 46% / 69%. On the shared window with honest minute detection (2015–2021) the sides agree nearly exactly (LANDED n 1,069 vs 1,060, +$24,747 vs +$22,935, PF 1.44 vs 1.41; OVERSHOT n 213 vs 216, −$30,570 vs −$28,346, PF 0.16 vs 0.20). Separations that reproduce: running entry-bar range ÷ ATR (death leg, both windows, both scopes) and penetration depth with its inversion within LANDED (deep landed trades are the best); completed-bar %K null on both sides. No feature yields an entry filter — within LANDED the same features point the other way. §J cross-check: forgone +$123,962 PF 2.57 (Harbor, replicated to the cent) vs author LANDED +$135,945 PF 2.45 — agree within ~10%; pure gentle core (LANDED ∩ forgone) 1,324 / +$149,547 / PF 4.08 **AGREED 2026-11-05** (packet 014_author): cells reproduce exactly from the extract, pairing protocol re-run from Harbor's raw files 0 mismatches, cross-feed label agreement 99.27% (minute) / 98.85% (M15), pure-core from author labels within 1% on matched subsets. W2 overshot share differs (31.1% Harbor vs 18.6% author M15) — noted, attributed to detection granularity + feed, not verdict-changing.
9. **(H1) forming-range cap: KILLED by mutual agreement 2026-11-05** (packets 006/007/008_author, 005_harbor) — static gain ~zero on both sides (+$2,061 PF flat; +$0.82/trade); no variant run. The timing-based candidate (entry timing within the bar) is a different hypothesis, definitions owed by Harbor.
10. **T1 delayed entry (Variant B, +15 min hold test): DEAD on both engines, both detections — first-touch stands; no delayed-entry follow-up under these criteria.** Harbor: B PF 0.794, −$17,425 vs A PF 1.152, +$35,076 (Dukascopy, full window). Author: minute B PF 0.82 vs A 0.94; M15 B PF 0.67 vs A 1.68. Ran-away census agrees across feeds (skipped A-only ~1,204–1,213 trades, +$123–131k, PF ~2.4–2.5). Session-rule convention: entry requires the entry sub-bar's close before 16:50 (not merely its start). (Packets 010_harbor / 017_author / 014_harbor.)

12. **T2 helper-bar confirmation (Variant H, enter at first 15m close after touch iff close moved in trade direction): DEAD on both engines — CLOSED 2026-11-05.** Author: minute H PF 0.76 / −$16,526, M15 H PF 0.80 / −$30,607 (vs A 0.94 / 1.68). Harbor (Dukascopy, full window): H 1,540 / −$12,821 / PF 0.922 / OOS 0.574 vs A 2,226 / +$35,076 / PF 1.152 / OOS 0.828; fails PF, net, OOS in both windows; only EB guard passes. The filter works (skipped "against" PF 0.36; kept 84.6% of T1 B's ran-away winners at PF 2.54) but the entry premium (1.931 pts/trade, $107,854) exceeds what selection earns. Fourth sighting of the too-late-information shape, opposite polarity from T1: T1 kept the wrong trades; T2 keeps the right trades and pays too much for them. First-touch stands. (Packets 021_author / 020_harbor / 023_author / 023_harbor.)
13. **T3 early exit (Variant E, cut at +15 if not in profit vs entry price): DEAD on both engines — CLOSED 2026-11-05.** Author: minute E PF 0.90 / −$8,844; M15 E PF 1.76 / +$99,222 vs A 1.68 / +$99,246 — net loses by $24, so the net leg fails. Harbor (Dukascopy, full window): E 2,322 / +$35,264 / PF 1.170 / OOS 0.775 vs A 2,226 / +$35,076 / PF 1.152 / OOS 0.828; W1 fails (PF 0.883), W2 passes (1.378), OOS fails. The +$189 full-window gain is knock-on noise: matched-pair saving on early exits is −$956 (−$1.57/trade). The +15 test separates winners from losers (held PF 1.592, cut PF 0.378) but the +15 price is roughly the average eventual exit for losers, so recoveries cancel stop savings. Ran-away winners kept perfectly (1,187/1,204 identical). Fifth sighting of the too-late-information shape, opposite polarity from T2: T2 paid in entry price, T3 pays in foregone winners. (Packets 024_author / 022_harbor / 025_author / 025_harbor.)
14. **D1 dedup survivor (keeps NEWEST vs OLDEST): DONE on Harbor's engine 2026-11-05 — NOT an edge.** Premise correction: Harbor's A engine already keeps the NEWEST level (`dedup_last3` iterates newest-first; §E1), so D1-as-defined ≡ A exactly (2,226 trades, trade-for-trade). The reverse direction (OLDEST, the author's rule) loses to A on PF, net, and OOS at both detections (min PF 1.146 vs 1.152, OOS 0.799 vs 0.828; q15 PF 1.694 vs 1.710, OOS 1.196 vs 1.246); it passes only the W2 leg. Zone census: 29% of zone-book cells change levels, yet 84.6% of trades are shared with identical P&L on 1,877 — the survivor rule is bookkeeping, not an edge, and cannot explain the PF 2.76 vs 1.15 gap. (Packets 028_author / 029_harbor / 030_author / 032_harbor; REPORT_MTZB.md §W; Accepted and closed by the author side (packet 035_author) — D1 CLOSED both sides; the author's D1 run stays as a confirmation run after I1.)
- **15. I3 crash-through momentum entry as pre-registered: INVALID — fill artifact, not judgeable (author side, 2026-11-05).** Trigger was a STATE ("forming extreme beyond the far edge"), not an EVENT: it fired at the first minute of every new H1 bar against stale zones, booking fills the market had not traded at in days/months. As run: minute PF 40.75 / +$7,703,232; M15 PF 123.25 / +$37,935,702 — absurd on their face. Trades > 5pt from the market net 100.7% / 100.2% of total net; non-phantom trades LOSE (minute −$54,432; M15 −$85,282). Monthly leg is the artifact (median fill 210pt / 483pt from market). I3 is neither PASS nor DEAD. Correction I3b (event-based trigger: the forming extreme must cross the far edge DURING the entry bar; a bar that OPENS beyond does NOT signal) needs fresh Rule 1a pre-registration before any code runs; I3b keeps test #1's slot, I1 stays #2. (Author packet 037; report `reference/dzb_mtzb_i3_momentum.md`.) **Harbor side agrees (packet 036_harbor, REPORT_MTZB.md §V):** Harbor's literal run reproduced the artifact independently (minute +$43,806,065 / PF 98.66; q15 +$42,786,072 / PF 139.49; Monthly 73% of net) and Harbor's I3 run was stopped on this packet. A freshness-gated variant run on Harbor's side (prev-H1-bar gate; NOT I3b) is catastrophically dead (minute PF 0.247 / −$354,666; q15 PF 0.243) — evidence the momentum hypothesis is badly hurt, but I3b (event-based, no signal on open-beyond — Harbor's choice, packet 036 §3) still gets its own pre-registered run.

## Mandate (John, 2026-11-05)
Both sides work together to find a trading strategy with a real edge, and audit each other. Everything must be correct and real. Coordination: hypothesis-first, one idea at a time; independent implementation on both engines/feeds; bar for "edge" is beating baseline on PF, net AND OOS in both windows (2015–2021 and 2022–2026) plus tick validation; live system frozen unless a candidate clears that bar and John orders a change. (Packet 2026-11-05_002_author.md.)

- **Bridge latitude** (2026-11-05, packets 2026-11-05_003_harbor.md / 2026-11-05_005_author.md / 2026-11-05_007_author.md): either side may propose bridge improvements by packet; adopted on the other side's ack; the adopting side updates PROTOCOL.md / STATE.md. Rails unchanged: discussion/verification only, nothing executes on live/demo systems, no third-party contact, digests to TZ continue, daily auto-round circuit breaker stays unless both sides agree in packets to change it.

## Three-side protocol (packet 050_author, acked 046_harbor)
- Verdicts record agreement per side; a side that has not run a check is recorded "not run", never implied as agreeing. A verdict is joint when every side that ran the check agrees.
- Verdict numbers are taken by the proposing side at application time; on a concurrent collision the later application takes the next free number and notes the renumber in its packet.
- Fresh-system convergence needs all three sides' agreement; a side that has not run a component records "no objection on the evidence" rather than blocking; a side that has run it and disagrees blocks until resolved in packets. Ordinary test verdicts are decided by the sides that ran them.
- STATE edits: fresh-download + verify-on-re-download for all three sides; any clobber is repaired and flagged in the next packet.
- Each side refreshes its own heartbeat and watches the others' (stale > 3 min = flag TZ). The 15-min backstop crons stay until: a few clean packets delivered on all three sides, heartbeats fresh, no missed packets.
- Watch coverage (author 075, John's order): every side watches every other side's packets AND heartbeats — author watches Harbor + cc (hook + backstop, live 21:18 ET); cc watches Harbor + author (hook live 21:12 ET); Harbor watches author + cc (to be confirmed by Harbor).
- Idle rule (John's order, author 080): when a side has no pending work, it fills its lane — create testable ideas or run the research lane (math/patterns/systems, anything that could make this work); nobody idles. Work is claimed in packets + QUEUE.md.
- Packet numbering stays per-side. Work (incl. cc's lanes) is claimed in packets + QUEUE.md.
